prompt[[[Continue the STRATS project at C:\Users\Mubarak\Desktop\Stats.

This is a Forex strategy/context library that will later feed QMX (a separate trading platform being coded elsewhere). STRATS is a portable folder structure — not a software project, not a "human-reviewable operating system."

Read these first:
- .hermes/orchestration/round-3/01-strats-decision-brief-revised.md (mission + boundaries)
- .hermes/orchestration/round-3/02-strats-build-and-population-plan-revised.md (phases, reference only — not gospel)

Subagent outputs from the previous session are in the Hermes delegation cache:
C:\Users\Mubarak\AppData\Local\hermes\cache\delegation\subagent-summary-*.txt
These contain the folder layouts, strategy package model, decision ledger, mini-KB design, ingestion pipeline, and a plan critique. Read them.

Standing corrections (do not regress):
1. It's a folder structure, not a software project or operating system.
2. Use models already in Hermes. No OpenCode.
3. Persistent Hermes bots, not skills or external CLIs.
4. Extraction tools are ready: watch skill, MarkItDown, Firecrawl, Gemini web app.
5. No Grok. No Kimi K3. DeepSeek is text-only.
6. QMX owns runtime/execution/live state. STRATS owns portable research.
7. Browser: bots do breadth, main session does visible verification.
8. Stock-trader videos are not excluded, but original asset class and transfer assumptions must be recorded.

Open agenda — these were NOT agreed on. Brainstorm and agree before building:
1. Source hierarchy (how to organize sources)
2. Two-pass sorting (pre-ingestion cheap sort + post-extraction evidence sort)
3. Source taxonomy (content type / asset class / Forex transferability)
4. How bots actually populate the library (the pipeline — this is the end goal)
5. Bot roles and model assignments (which model for which role)
6. OpenKnowledge vs Obsidian (whether to use OK as editing layer)

Don't over-engineer. Don't pre-decide the open items. Let's brainstorm.]]]]

| [skills/watch](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/tree/main/skills/watch "This path skips through empty directories") | [skills/watch](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/tree/main/skills/watch "This path skips through empty directories") | [Fix WATCH\_DETAIL silently falling back to default](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/commit/83da59fa78c3eee9e20f515fe75c438bb5166efd "Fix WATCH_DETAIL silently falling back to default  An inline comment on the WATCH_DETAIL value line (e.g. `WATCH_DETAIL=balanced  # ...`) parsed as the literal \"balanced  # ...\" value, failed validation, and silently reverted to the default mode.  - setup.py: move the allowed-values list off the value line in the env   template so the wizard no longer models a trailing inline comment. - config.py: strip a whitespace-preceded inline comment from unquoted   values so a stray comment can't silently break selection again   (preserves '#' inside quotes / API keys). - SKILL.md: note to write the bare key with no trailing comment. │       ├── whisper.py            # Groq / OpenAI clients (pure stdlib)
│       ├── config.py             # shared config (~/.config/watch/.env)
│       ├── setup.py              # preflight + installer
│       └── build-skill.sh        # build dist/watch.skill for claude.ai upload (dev-only)
├── hooks/                        # SessionStart status hook (Claude Code only)
├── .claude-plugin/               # plugin.json + marketplace.json (Claude Code)
├── .codex-plugin/                # plugin.json — Codex/agents manifest ("skills": "./skills/")
├── .agents/plugins/              # marketplace.json — Agent Skills marketplace listing
├── AGENTS.md → CLAUDE.md         # generic-agent entry point
├── tests/                        # pytest suite (ffmpeg-synthesized clips, no network)
└── .github/workflows/            # release.yml — auto-builds watch.skill on tag push

## Develop

[Permalink: Develop](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video#develop)

```
# Run the test suite (stdlib + pytest; ffmpeg required for frame tests):
python3 -m pytest -q

# Build the claude.ai upload bundle:
bash skills/watch/scripts/build-skill.sh      # → dist/watch.skill
```

Releasing: tag `vX.Y.Z`, push the tag. The workflow builds `dist/watch.skill` and attaches it to the GitHub release. Keep the version in sync across `skills/watch/SKILL.md`, `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, and `.codex-plugin/plugin.json`.

See [CHANGELOG.md](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md) for version history.

## Open source

[Permalink: Open source](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video#open-source)

MIT license.

Built on `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, and Claude's multimodal `Read` tool. Whisper transcription via [Groq](https://groq.com/) or [OpenAI](https://openai.com/).

Built by Brad Bonanno — I make content about building with AI on [YouTube (@bradbonanno)](https://www.youtube.com/@bradbonanno), and build AI operating systems for businesses at [Solaris Automation](https://www.solarisautomation.io/). If `/watch` saves you from scrubbing through a video, come say hi on the channel.

**Citation key:** `M#` = actual `messages.id` in session `20260824_121737_86f88c`; `TL#–#` = line range in `transcripts-lineage/20260824_121737_86f88c-friendly-greeting.md`.

## 1. Recovered model

The original idea began as four human-facing pillars:

1. **Level/location** — where attention or eligibility is focused.
2. **Trigger** — the event authorizing entry.
3. **Confirmation/filter** — supporting or gating evidence.
4. **Risk/management** — initially risk and sizing, later corrected and repartitioned.

The user stressed that every pillar could contain **any number of components** and was “not finite” (`M779`). The assistant then normalized this into the enduring model:

```text
Source evidence / atomic claims
        ↓
Reusable primitive dictionary
        ↓
Contextual role bindings
        ↓
Typed strategy logic graph
        ↓
Source-faithful candidate or entry_hypothesis package
        ↓
QMX implementation, testing, optimization, and runtime governance
```

The decisive formulation was:

> “The true reusable unit is: `primitive + role binding + timing + parameters + scope + evidence + data provenance`.” (`M795`)

And later:

> “1. Primitive dictionary — ‘What things exist?’  
> 2. Role bindings — ‘What job does this primitive perform here?’  
> 3. Strategy logic graph — ‘How are the bound components combined?’” (`M831`)

## Bottom line

**Use `claude-video/watch` as a bounded, multimodal evidence probe inside a Hermes worker—not as STRATS’ ingestion backbone.** It is useful for turning selected video windows into timestamped transcript plus inspectable frames. It does **not** provide durable batch intake, resumability, multilingual caption management, long-video chunking for an LLM, evidence manifests, OCR/chart reconstruction, or trading-rule normalization.

The repository’s own guidance is candid: **“Best accuracy: videos under 10 minutes.”**[2] That makes full-pass analysis of 3–5-hour podcasts the wrong default; targeted windows are the right role.

## Exactly what it does

`/watch` is an Agent Skill around a pure-stdlib Python pipeline:

1. Accepts **one** public URL supported by `yt-dlp`, or one local video.
2. Checks metadata and English captions.
3. Downloads audio or video if required.
4. Extracts timestamped JPEG frames.
5. Uses native captions, or uploads extracted audio to Groq/OpenAI Whisper.
6. Prints a Markdown report.
7. Instructs the hosting agent to open every JPEG with its image/vision tool and answer the user.[1][2][3]

The user’s question is handled by the **agent**, not `watch.py`; the script only produces evidence. It does not call Claude for vision. The only model APIs inside the code are optional Whisper endpoints.

### Architecture and dependencies

- **Runtime:** Python standard library.
- **External binaries:** `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`.

prompt[[[Continue the STRATS project at C:\Users\Mubarak\Desktop\Stats.

This is a Forex strategy/context library that will later feed QMX (a separate trading platform being coded elsewhere). STRATS is a portable folder structure — not a software project, not a "human-reviewable operating system."

Read these first:
- .hermes/orchestration/round-3/01-strats-decision-brief-revised.md (mission + boundaries)
- .hermes/orchestration/round-3/02-strats-build-and-population-plan-revised.md (phases, reference only — not gospel)

Subagent outputs from the previous session are in the Hermes delegation cache:
C:\Users\Mubarak\AppData\Local\hermes\cache\delegation\subagent-summary-*.txt
These contain the folder layouts, strategy package model, decision ledger, mini-KB design, ingestion pipeline, and a plan critique. Read them.

Standing corrections (do not regress):
1. It's a folder structure, not a software project or operating system.
2. Use models already in Hermes. No OpenCode.
3. Persistent Hermes bots, not skills or external CLIs.
4. Extraction tools are ready: watch skill, MarkItDown, Firecrawl, Gemini web app.
5. No Grok. No Kimi K3. DeepSeek is text-only.
6. QMX owns runtime/execution/live state. STRATS owns portable research.
7. Browser: bots do breadth, main session does visible verification.
8. Stock-trader videos are not excluded, but original asset class and transfer assumptions must be recorded.

Open agenda — these were NOT agreed on. Brainstorm and agree before building:
1. Source hierarchy (how to organize sources)
2. Two-pass sorting (pre-ingestion cheap sort + post-extraction evidence sort)
3. Source taxonomy (content type / asset class / Forex transferability)
4. How bots actually populate the library (the pipeline — this is the end goal)
5. Bot roles and model assignments (which model for which role)
6. OpenKnowledge vs Obsidian (whether to use OK as editing layer)

Don't over-engineer. Don't pre-decide the open items. Let's brainstorm.]]]]

| [skills/watch](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/tree/main/skills/watch "This path skips through empty directories") | [skills/watch](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/tree/main/skills/watch "This path skips through empty directories") | [Fix WATCH\_DETAIL silently falling back to default](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/commit/83da59fa78c3eee9e20f515fe75c438bb5166efd "Fix WATCH_DETAIL silently falling back to default  An inline comment on the WATCH_DETAIL value line (e.g. `WATCH_DETAIL=balanced  # ...`) parsed as the literal \"balanced  # ...\" value, failed validation, and silently reverted to the default mode.  - setup.py: move the allowed-values list off the value line in the env   template so the wizard no longer models a trailing inline comment. - config.py: strip a whitespace-preceded inline comment from unquoted   values so a stray comment can't silently break selection again   (preserves '#' inside quotes / API keys). - SKILL.md: note to write the bare key with no trailing comment. ## Develop

[Permalink: Develop](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video#develop)

```
# Run the test suite (stdlib + pytest; ffmpeg required for frame tests):
python3 -m pytest -q

# Build the claude.ai upload bundle:
bash skills/watch/scripts/build-skill.sh      # → dist/watch.skill
```

Releasing: tag `vX.Y.Z`, push the tag. The workflow builds `dist/watch.skill` and attaches it to the GitHub release. Keep the version in sync across `skills/watch/SKILL.md`, `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, and `.codex-plugin/plugin.json`.

See [CHANGELOG.md](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md) for version history.

## Open source

[Permalink: Open source](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video#open-source)

MIT license.

Built on `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, and Claude's multimodal `Read` tool. Whisper transcription via [Groq](https://groq.com/) or [OpenAI](https://openai.com/).

Built by Brad Bonanno — I make content about building with AI on [YouTube (@bradbonanno)](https://www.youtube.com/@bradbonanno), and build AI operating systems for businesses at [Solaris Automation](https://www.solarisautomation.io/). If `/watch` saves you from scrubbing through a video, come say hi on the channel.

# STRATS recovery dossier

**Citation key:** `M#` = actual `messages.id` in session `20260824_121737_86f88c`; `TL#–#` = line range in `transcripts-lineage/20260824_121737_86f88c-friendly-greeting.md`.

## 1. Recovered model

The original idea began as four human-facing pillars:

1. **Level/location** — where attention or eligibility is focused.
2. **Trigger** — the event authorizing entry.
3. **Confirmation/filter** — supporting or gating evidence.
4. **Risk/management** — initially risk and sizing, later corrected and repartitioned.

The user stressed that every pillar could contain **any number of components** and was “not finite” (`M779`). The assistant then normalized this into the enduring model:

```text
Source evidence / atomic claims
        ↓
Reusable primitive dictionary
        ↓
Contextual role bindings
        ↓
Typed strategy logic graph
        ↓
Source-faithful candidate or entry_hypothesis package
        ↓
QMX implementation, testing, optimization, and runtime governance
```

The decisive formulation was:

> “The true reusable unit is: `primitive + role binding + timing + parameters + scope + evidence + data provenance`.” (`M795`)

And later:

> “1. Primitive dictionary — ‘What things exist?’  
> 2. Role bindings — ‘What job does this primitive perform here?’  
> 3. Strategy logic graph — ‘How are the bound components combined?’” (`M831`)

## Bottom line

**Use `claude-video/watch` as a bounded, multimodal evidence probe inside a Hermes worker—not as STRATS’ ingestion backbone.** It is useful for turning selected video windows into timestamped transcript plus inspectable frames. It does **not** provide durable batch intake, resumability, multilingual caption management, long-video chunking for an LLM, evidence manifests, OCR/chart reconstruction, or trading-rule normalization.

The repository’s own guidance is candid: **“Best accuracy: videos under 10 minutes.”**[2] That makes full-pass analysis of 3–5-hour podcasts the wrong default; targeted windows are the right role.

## Exactly what it does

`/watch` is an Agent Skill around a pure-stdlib Python pipeline:

1. Accepts **one** public URL supported by `yt-dlp`, or one local video.
2. Checks metadata and English captions.
3. Downloads audio or video if required.
4. Extracts timestamped JPEG frames.
5. Uses native captions, or uploads extracted audio to Groq/OpenAI Whisper.
6. Prints a Markdown report.
7. Instructs the hosting agent to open every JPEG with its image/vision tool and answer the user.[1][2][3]

The user’s question is handled by the **agent**, not `watch.py`; the script only produces evidence. It does not call Claude for vision. The only model APIs inside the code are optional Whisper endpoints.

### Architecture and dependencies

- **Runtime:** Python standard library.
- **External binaries:** `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`.

prompt[[[Continue the STRATS project at C:\Users\Mubarak\Desktop\Stats.

This is a Forex strategy/context library that will later feed QMX (a separate trading platform being coded elsewhere). STRATS is a portable folder structure — not a software project, not a "human-reviewable operating system."

Read these first:
- .hermes/orchestration/round-3/01-strats-decision-brief-revised.md (mission + boundaries)
- .hermes/orchestration/round-3/02-strats-build-and-population-plan-revised.md (phases, reference only — not gospel)

Subagent outputs from the previous session are in the Hermes delegation cache:
C:\Users\Mubarak\AppData\Local\hermes\cache\delegation\subagent-summary-*.txt
These contain the folder layouts, strategy package model, decision ledger, mini-KB design, ingestion pipeline, and a plan critique. Read them.

Standing corrections (do not regress):
1. It's a folder structure, not a software project or operating system.
2. Use models already in Hermes. No OpenCode.
3. Persistent Hermes bots, not skills or external CLIs.
4. Extraction tools are ready: watch skill, MarkItDown, Firecrawl, Gemini web app.
5. No Grok. No Kimi K3. DeepSeek is text-only.
6. QMX owns runtime/execution/live state. STRATS owns portable research.
7. Browser: bots do breadth, main session does visible verification.
8. Stock-trader videos are not excluded, but original asset class and transfer assumptions must be recorded.

Open agenda — these were NOT agreed on. Brainstorm and agree before building:
1. Source hierarchy (how to organize sources)
2. Two-pass sorting (pre-ingestion cheap sort + post-extraction evidence sort)
3. Source taxonomy (content type / asset class / Forex transferability)
4. How bots actually populate the library (the pipeline — this is the end goal)
5. Bot roles and model assignments (which model for which role)
6. OpenKnowledge vs Obsidian (whether to use OK as editing layer)

Don't over-engineer. Don't pre-decide the open items. Let's brainstorm.]]]]

## Develop

[Permalink: Develop](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video#develop)

```
# Run the test suite (stdlib + pytest; ffmpeg required for frame tests):
python3 -m pytest -q

# Build the claude.ai upload bundle:
bash skills/watch/scripts/build-skill.sh      # → dist/watch.skill
```

Releasing: tag `vX.Y.Z`, push the tag. The workflow builds `dist/watch.skill` and attaches it to the GitHub release. Keep the version in sync across `skills/watch/SKILL.md`, `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, and `.codex-plugin/plugin.json`.

See [CHANGELOG.md](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video/blob/main/CHANGELOG.md) for version history.

## Open source

[Permalink: Open source](https://github.com/bradautomates/claude-video#open-source)

MIT license.

Built on `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, and Claude's multimodal `Read` tool. Whisper transcription via [Groq](https://groq.com/) or [OpenAI](https://openai.com/).

Built by Brad Bonanno — I make content about building with AI on [YouTube (@bradbonanno)](https://www.youtube.com/@bradbonanno), and build AI operating systems for businesses at [Solaris Automation](https://www.solarisautomation.io/). If `/watch` saves you from scrubbing through a video, come say hi on the channel.

# STRATS recovery dossier

**Citation key:** `M#` = actual `messages.id` in session `20260824_121737_86f88c`; `TL#–#` = line range in `transcripts-lineage/20260824_121737_86f88c-friendly-greeting.md`.

## 1. Recovered model

The original idea began as four human-facing pillars:

1. **Level/location** — where attention or eligibility is focused.
2. **Trigger** — the event authorizing entry.
3. **Confirmation/filter** — supporting or gating evidence.
4. **Risk/management** — initially risk and sizing, later corrected and repartitioned.

The user stressed that every pillar could contain **any number of components** and was “not finite” (`M779`). The assistant then normalized this into the enduring model:

```text
Source evidence / atomic claims
        ↓
Reusable primitive dictionary
        ↓
Contextual role bindings
        ↓
Typed strategy logic graph
        ↓
Source-faithful candidate or entry_hypothesis package
        ↓
QMX implementation, testing, optimization, and runtime governance
```

The decisive formulation was:

> “The true reusable unit is: `primitive + role binding + timing + parameters + scope + evidence + data provenance`.” (`M795`)

And later:

> “1. Primitive dictionary — ‘What things exist?’  
> 2. Role bindings — ‘What job does this primitive perform here?’  
> 3. Strategy logic graph — ‘How are the bound components combined?’” (`M831`)

## Bottom line

**Use `claude-video/watch` as a bounded, multimodal evidence probe inside a Hermes worker—not as STRATS’ ingestion backbone.** It is useful for turning selected video windows into timestamped transcript plus inspectable frames. It does **not** provide durable batch intake, resumability, multilingual caption management, long-video chunking for an LLM, evidence manifests, OCR/chart reconstruction, or trading-rule normalization.

The repository’s own guidance is candid: **“Best accuracy: videos under 10 minutes.”**[2] That makes full-pass analysis of 3–5-hour podcasts the wrong default; targeted windows are the right role.

## Exactly what it does

`/watch` is an Agent Skill around a pure-stdlib Python pipeline:

1. Accepts **one** public URL supported by `yt-dlp`, or one local video.
2. Checks metadata and English captions.
3. Downloads audio or video if required.
4. Extracts timestamped JPEG frames.
5. Uses native captions, or uploads extracted audio to Groq/OpenAI Whisper.
6. Prints a Markdown report.
7. Instructs the hosting agent to open every JPEG with its image/vision tool and answer the user.[1][2][3]

The user’s question is handled by the **agent**, not `watch.py`; the script only produces evidence. It does not call Claude for vision. The only model APIs inside the code are optional Whisper endpoints.

### Architecture and dependencies

- **Runtime:** Python standard library.
- **External binaries:** `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`.

## Bottom line

**Use `claude-video/watch` as a bounded, multimodal evidence probe inside a Hermes worker—not as STRATS’ ingestion backbone.** It is useful for turning selected video windows into timestamped transcript plus inspectable frames. It does **not** provide durable batch intake, resumability, multilingual caption management, long-video chunking for an LLM, evidence manifests, OCR/chart reconstruction, or trading-rule normalization.

The repository’s own guidance is candid: **“Best accuracy: videos under 10 minutes.”**[2] That makes full-pass analysis of 3–5-hour podcasts the wrong default; targeted windows are the right role.

## Exactly what it does

`/watch` is an Agent Skill around a pure-stdlib Python pipeline:

1. Accepts **one** public URL supported by `yt-dlp`, or one local video.
2. Checks metadata and English captions.
3. Downloads audio or video if required.
4. Extracts timestamped JPEG frames.
5. Uses native captions, or uploads extracted audio to Groq/OpenAI Whisper.
6. Prints a Markdown report.
7. Instructs the hosting agent to open every JPEG with its image/vision tool and answer the user.[1][2][3]

The user’s question is handled by the **agent**, not `watch.py`; the script only produces evidence. It does not call Claude for vision. The only model APIs inside the code are optional Whisper endpoints.

### Architecture and dependencies

- **Runtime:** Python standard library.
- **External binaries:** `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`.

## Key Decisions

1. STRATS is a **portable Forex strategy/context knowledge base / folder structure**, not a software project.
   - Reason: user repeatedly corrected compaction drift.
2. QMX is separate and later.
   - Reason: QMX owns runtime, backtesting, evolution, execution, experiments, database/runtime state, and software machinery.
3. STRATS should store evidence-backed **specifications**, not executable bots.
   - Reason: STRATS is upstream research/context; QMX later implements/tests/optimizes.
4. QMX owns:
   - trading-bot implementations;
   - executable test manifests;
   - experiment workspaces/ledgers;
   - executors;
   - run results;
   - evolution;
   - adapters;
   - runtime/software locks.
5. STRATS owns:
   - source evidence;
   - atomic claims;
   - primitives;
   - role bindings;
   - logic graphs;
   - source-faithful strategy/spec packages;
   - mini-KBs;
   - durable logical lineage/catalog.
6. The recovered trading dictionary model is central.
   - Reason: user is stronger in dev work than trading and needs consistent trading vocabulary for future spec-driven QMX work.
7. Do not silently choose between recovered dictionary and newer completeness candidates.
   - Reason: delegation task explicitly compared keep/add/merge/remove decisions without picking a winner.
8. `claude-video` is potentially useful as an evidence extractor, not a complete reasoning system.
   - Reason: repo downloads videos, extracts frames/transcripts, and passes evidence to Claude; the agent/model still handles interpretation.

## Key Decisions
1. STRATS is a **portable plain-file/Obsidian trading-strategy knowledge library**, not software.
   - Reason: user repeatedly corrected compaction/agent drift: “This entire thing is not code—it’s just a folder.”
2. QMX is now out of scope for current STRATS work.
   - Reason: latest user decided QMX will adapt to the library later: “QMX will simply adopt and it shall update.”
3. Do not inspect QMX GitBook or design around QMX for the dictionary work unless user explicitly reverses this.
   - Reason: user almost provided GitBook context then chose to leave QMX out.
4. Dictionary/primitive model is central and should be kept.
   - Reason: both original recovery and latest audit say primitive dictionary → contextual role binding → strategy logic graph was assistant-proposed, positively received, and now explicitly confirmed.
5. The dictionary was designed but not populated.
   - Reason: no actual canonical dictionary files or source-derived primitive entries were created; only examples/design/taxonomy existed.
6. Keep primitive independent from role.
   - Reason: same primitive can serve as level/location, trigger, confirmation/filter, exit/invalidation/management depending on strategy context.
7. Human-facing pillars are useful review/completeness lenses, not a rigid ontology.
   - Reason: user emphasized pillars/components were not finite.
8. Missing exits require labelled authority/completeness, not automatic rejection.
   - Reason: latest corrections and skill patches established missing exit can be `source_defined`, `external_policy`, `deliberately_open`, or `unresolved`; do not invent or misattribute exits.
9. Remove mandatory “missing exit = entry_hypothesis” as a universal rule.
   - Reason: audit found prior recovery/dossier overstated this; original transcript liked entry hypothesis as a useful capture idea, not mandatory classification for every missing exit.
10. Remove QMX-derived fields/processes from current STRATS dictionary/spec design.
   - Reason: user said QMX will adapt later and STRATS must stay independent.
11. Model routing should be explicit and task-sensitive.
   - Reason: user noticed possible GPT-5.6-Sol use and prefers reserving stronger models for tasks that need them, using DeepSeek/GLM/cheaper suitable models for ordinary text work.
12. No Grok and no Kimi K3 for now.
   - Reason: user explicitly excluded them.
13. Orchestration is a working method, not a STRATS architecture layer.
   - Reason: user wants context-preserving delegation but warns strongly against overengineering.
14. `claude-video` is useful as an evidence extractor only.
   - Reason: inspected repo downloads/extracts frames/transcripts and hands evidence to Claude; it does not itself perform full trading/video reasoning.

## Resolved Questions
1. Was the “Continue the STRATS project...” prompt authored by the user?
   - Answer: No. User clarified it came from another recovery session and should not be treated as direct authority.
2. Was a concrete dictionary populated?
   - Answer: No. The latest audit and previous recovery agree: only the concept, purpose, relationship to strategies, starter families/examples, attributes, and agent usage pattern were designed; no canonical dictionary files or source-derived entries were created.
3. What does “not populated” mean?
   - Answer: no actual dictionary files, no canonical primitive entries, no source-derived population, no alias/deduplication work. The dictionary model exists; the library content does not yet.
4. Was primitive → role binding → logic graph genuinely accepted?
   - Answer: Yes, with nuance. It was assistant-proposed, positively received, and now explicitly confirmed as the semantic core. Exact field syntax/graph keywords/package formats were not all approved.
5. What is a primitive?
   - Answer: a reusable trading concept/condition/event/object/action such as bullish engulfing, liquidity sweep, RSI crossing, order block, displacement, ATR stop, time exit, DOM imbalance, break of structure.
6. Is a primitive permanently a trigger/confirmation/etc.?
   - Answer: No. Role comes from the contextual binding in a specific strategy.
7. What is a role binding?
   - Answer: a contextual statement of what job a primitive performs in a particular setup, including role, parameters/profile, scope, timing, direction, timeframe/session, evidence/provenance, and relationship to other components.
8. What is a strategy logic graph?
   - Answer: the representation of how bound components combine: order, conditions, Boolean/temporal relations, entry semantics, invalidation, management/exits if source-defined or otherwise labelled.
9. What are the human-facing pillars?
   - Answer: level/location, trigger, confirmation/filter/confluence, exits/invalidation/management. They are useful lenses/checklists, not fixed primitive categories and not finite.
10. How should missing exits be handled now?
    - Answer: label exit authority/completeness; do not automatically reject; do not silently invent; possible statuses include `source_defined`, `external_policy`, `deliberately_open`, `unresolved`.
11. Should QMX GitBook be inspected now?
    - Answer: No. User provided the link but then decided to leave QMX out; QMX will adapt later.
12. What does `claude-video` do at a high level?
    - Answer: it gives Claude a `/watch` workflow that downloads video, extracts frames, transcribes, and hands evidence to Claude.
13. Does `watch.py` itself answer video questions?
    - Answer: No. It produces evidence; the hosting agent/model answers using the evidence. Optional model APIs inside code are Whisper endpoints.
14. What are `claude-video` runtime dependencies?
    - Answer: Python standard library plus external binaries `yt-dlp`, `ffmpeg`, `ffprobe`.
15. Did `deleg_7c53330b` complete?
    - Answer: Yes. 1/1 subagent completed successfully in ~356.23s total elapsed, `api_calls=9`, model shown as `?`.
16. Did the `deleg_7c53330b` audit create or modify files?
    - Answer: No, according to its report: “Files created or modified: none.”
17. What issue did `deleg_7c53330b` find in the prior recovery dossier?
    - Answer: two supposed direct quotes were not verbatim transcript text, and some exact syntax/field approvals were overstated.
